## Document 097: 2026年・冬のボーナス予測 ～前年比＋3.0％と６年連続の増加を予想～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年・冬のボーナス予測 ～前年比＋3.0％と６年連続の増加を予想～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年 11 月 6 日

1/2 
2026 年・冬のボーナス予測 
～前年比＋3.0％と６年連続の増加を予想～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

○ 民間企業の2026 年冬のボーナス一人当たり支給額を前年比＋3.0％（昨冬同＋2.8％）と予想する
（厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計」ベース）。冬のボーナスとしては６年連続の増加となるだろう。中
東情勢の悪化に伴う資源価格上昇が企業活動の重荷となるなかでも企業収益は高水準を維持してい
る。人手不足を背景とした処遇改善の動きも根強く、今冬もボーナスの増加が続くと予想する。

○ 11 月６日に厚生労働省から公表された26 年夏のボーナス（賞与支給事業所における労働者一人平
均賞与額）は、前年比＋5.3％と５年連続で増加し、昨夏の同＋2.9％から伸びも拡大した。また、
賞与を支給していない事業所も含む「全事業所における労働者一人平均賞与額」で見ても同＋
5.9％と増加している。賞与を支給する事業所の割合も前年の 73.4％から74.0％へ上昇しており、
企業の従業員への還元姿勢が強いことがうかがえる。

○ ボーナスの交渉では、春闘時にその年の年間賞与を決定する夏冬型が採用されていることが多い。
2026 年春闘では、月例給与だけでなく一時金でも増額回答が目立った。企業収益が高水準にある
ことに加え、物価高への配慮や人材確保の観点から、賞与を含めた処遇改善を進める企業が多かっ
たとみられる。また、月例給与の引き上げが賞与の算定基礎額を押し上げ、支給月数の増加と相ま
ってボーナス増につながる面もある。夏のボーナス増に続き、冬についてもこうした交渉結果が反
映される形で増加が予想される。

○ 毎期型の企業、あるいは労働組合が存在しない中小・零細企業においては、より直近の収益状況・
業況がボーナスに反映されやすい。もっとも、イラン情勢悪化によるコスト上昇が企業収益の下押
し要因となるなかでも、価格転嫁の進展などを背景として企業収益は総じて底堅く推移している。
利益の水準も高く、従業員への還元余力はなお保たれているとみてよいだろう。

○ 人手不足感が強いことも、ボーナス増加を後押しする。企業にとって人材の確保・定着は引き続き
重要な経営課題となっており、月例給与だけでなく賞与を含めた処遇改善の必要性は高い。物価高
によって家計負担が増加するなか、従業員への還元を求める声も強い。こうした点も今冬のボーナ
ス増加を支える要因となる。

○ 労務行政研究所が９月30 日に公表した調査によれば、東証プライム上場企業 175 社の 2026 年年末

### Page 2

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
2/2 
一時金妥結額は、前年比＋4.2％と５年連続で増加した。製造業が同＋4.3％、非製造業が同＋
3.5％と、いずれもプラスとなっている。大企業中心の調査であり、中小企業やパートタイム労働
者も含む毎月勤労統計とは対象が異なるものの、冬のボーナスも堅調に増加するという見方を裏付
ける結果といえる。

○ 冬のボーナス増加は家計の所得環境にとって好材料であり、個人消費を下支えすることが期待され
る。一方で懸念されるのが物価上昇の影響である。中東情勢悪化に伴うコスト上昇の価格転嫁など
を背景として、今後は物価上昇率が高まる可能性が高く、政府による電気・ガス代支援の効果を織
り込んでも、2027 年初にはＣＰＩコアの前年比上昇率が＋３％台に達することが予想される。物
価上昇による家計の購買力の圧迫が続くなか、ボーナス増加による個人消費の押し上げ効果につい
ては過度な期待は禁物だろう。

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

AWSC’s 3Q26 core business OP was NT$209mn, which was lower 
than both GSe/BBG consensus at NT$226mn/NT$250mn, mainly 
due to the ongoing increase in raw material cost, leading to a 24.3% 
GM (lower than GSe/BBG consensus GM at 25.6%/27.8%), despite 
UTR in 3Q26 remaining stable at 65-70%. Moreover, AWSC reported 
NI of NT$191mn, which was 3% higher than GSe but 17% lower than 
the BBG consensus due to the GM contraction. 
Going into 4Q26, we believe the company’s revenue will increase by 
1% as key business lines (including WiFi, cellular and VCSEL) should 
remain stable in 4Q26 as we expect the company’s utilization rate to 
remain stable at 65-70%. Moreover, we expect its GM to rise to 
27.2% (up 2.9ppt QoQ) due to the material cost increase slowing 
down. 
We continue to view rising material costs as the key risk to AWSC’s 
profitability, as the company is now securing raw materials at higher 
cost as low-cost inventory has already been depleted in 1H26 amid 
the strong demand pull in. The company continues to negotiate 
price increases with clients and expects the adjustments to be fully 
reflected starting in 1Q27. However, we remain cautious on the 
low-end cellular segment, where customer demand tends to be 
more price-sensitive. 
For the AI-related business, the company continues to ship VCSEL 
products for 400G/800G optical transceiver this year, with revenue 
contribution to be <2% in 2026E per our estimates. Meanwhile, the 
company continues to make progress on the new PD (Photodiode) 
product and is currently undergoing verification with several 
customers. The company expects PD products to start seeing 
revenue contribution starting from 2H27 or 2027-end given its long 
verification process, typically 6 months+, thus we expect revenue 
contribution to be limited at <2%/~5% in 2027E/2028E. 
Investment view

### Page 3

We maintain our conservative view on the cellular/WiFi PA industry (see here) as we 
continue to see weaker smartphone shipment (-10%/+2%/+2% in 2026/27/28, see 
here) as the smartphone market continues to be impacted by the elevated memory price 
(see here). 
For AWSC, we believe the company could further gain market share among China PA 
design houses as Chinese clients increasingly shift toward higher-end PA products, 
supported by the company’s superior cost control capability and higher production yield 
relative to peers. In addition, AWSC has continued to expand its market share with a 
Korean smartphone brand, which should provide further growth momentum to its 
cellular business in coming quarters/years. Overall, we expect AWSC to continue gaining 
share from its key competitors due to (1) its competitive pricing strategy, underpinned 
by a more favorable cost structure comparing to its key competitor, Win Semi 
(AWSC/Win Semi’s fixed cost accounts for 30%/40% of the total, while Win Semi’s COGS 
per wafer is ~130% higher than AWSC’s) and (2) its strategic focus on China- and 
US-based PA design houses. 
We continue to see a recovery in 2026 CAPEX plans among Taiwan PA foundry players. 
However, we do not view this as a signal of a meaningfully stronger demand outlook as 
the capital spending is more related to new AI businesses. AWSC revised up its 2026 
CAPEX guidance to NT$600-650mn (from NT$500-600mn) and continues to expect 
CAPEX to increase in 2027/28. Also, management continues to expect UTR to reach at 
most ~70% in 2026, implying no need for capacity expansion in the near term while the 
additional capex will be spent on new optical related business. 
Overall, we maintain our Neutral rating on AWSC with a new 12-m TP of NT$136 (from 
NT$147) as we roll over our valuation period by one quarter. Post results, we revise 
down our 2027E/28E earnings by 10%/10% to reflect lower revenue and the negative 
impact from rising material cost (GM contraction), revise up 2026E earnings by 2%, and 
introduce our 2029E estimates.

### Page 5

Earnings revisions  
We revise up our 2026E revenue by 1% to factor in the 3Q26 actual results and we revise 
down 2027E/28E revenue by 9%/10% assuming a more conservative outlook for the 
cellular business; we revise down 2026E/27E/28E GM estimates by 0.4/0.7/0.8ppt to 
reflect the negative impact from rising material cost. Overall, we revise down 2027E/28E 
earnings by 10%/10% and revise up 2026E earnings by 2%. 
In this report, we introduce our 2029E estimates.

Valuation 
We maintain our Neutral rating and revise down our TP from NT$147 to NT$136, based 
on a lower 25x target P/E (from 27x previously, 0.5 STDV below the company’s past 
10-year forward P/E multiple), factoring in our earnings revisions and, consistent with 
our group coverage practices, rolling over our valuation period from 4Q26-3Q27E to 
2027E.

### Page 6

Investment Thesis and TP & risks

AWSC is one of the largest GaAS foundries globally, with 10-15% market share in the 
GaAS foundry industry in 2025. We expect the company to enjoy solid share gain 
opportunity in the long term, considering its much lower than peers’ production cost 
and better production efficiency. However, given the high inventory level in the supply 
chain, and the slower-than-expected Android smartphone PA demand in coming 
quarters, we expect relatively slow profit growth in the near term. 
We are Neutral rated given the company’s relatively slow profit growth in coming 
quarters. We think the stock is priced fairly, trading at its historical upcycle average P/E 
multiple, despite the long-term market share growth opportunity. We forecast AWSC’s 
net income to grow at a 34% 2026E-28E CAGR. The key up/downside risks are (1) 
easing/rising competition in the China PA foundry market, (2) 
faster/slower-than-expected progress on 5G/WiFi 7 upgrade trends, and (3) 
faster/slower-than expected progress with new businesses, including LEO satellite, 
drones and datacom.  
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$136 is based on a target 25x 
2027E P/E, which is 0.5 STDV below the company’s past 10-year forward P/E multiple 
and the inventory build-up period average P/E. We consider this multiple appropriate 
given the soft demand outlook and our positive view on the company’s longer-term 
share gain opportunity. 
The key up/downside risks are 1) easing/rising competition in the China PA foundry 
market, (2) faster/slower-than-expected progress on 5G/WiFi 7 upgrade trends, and (3) 
faster/slower-than expected progress with new businesses, including LEO satellite, 
drones and datacom.

• Anticipating another strong year into 2027 — MPI has maintained its 
current guidance for capacity plan into next year: year end-2026 capacity 
targets of VPC 2m and MEMS 3.5m, and 2027 MEMS expansion is expected 
to be at least another 2.5m. However, we see potential upside thanks to 
robust customer demand and market share gain. CPO insertion 2 is under 
final evaluation and insertion 3 already starts engineering  shipments  to 
more than 5 international customers across foundry, OSAT and fabless 
segments. The company sees strong growth momentum of probe card into 
next year and CPO equipment contribution at single digit in 2027 with more 
upside into 2028.
• We stay constructive on MPI despite recent market concerns regarding AI 
demand slowing down. The key drivers are continuing to ramp-up at 
existing AI ASIC customers, incremental contributions from new AI/non-AI 
ASIC and CPU customers, further MEMS capacity expansion, and potential 
upside from CPO and microbump probe-card technology.

2
MPI
Valuation
Our target price for MPI is set at NT$7,200, based on a target PE multiple of 52x applied to our 2027-28E EPS average. We
believe our PE target, at the high end of MPI's 3-year forward PE range, is justified given MPI’s earnings power remains
structurally higher than that in the previous cycles, broadening demand across AI ASIC, additional CPU opportunities and non-
AI ASIC wins. We expect MPI to see accelerating earnings growth ahead supported by sustainable sales growth and an

improving mix. At our target price, the shares would trade at 2027E/28E P/B of 23x/15x.
Risks
Our quant risk rating system rates the MPI stock as High Risk for its share-price volatility. Key downside risks that could
prevent MPI shares from reaching our target price include 1) slowdown in AI ASIC demand; 2) slower-than-expected adoption
of MEMS probe cards; 3) intensifying competition from global and regional peers; 4) slow development of the CPO equipment

business.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

力旺(3529)：2H26 權利金大幅成長、Security 長期機會大 
建議：維持買進

一、2026 年前三季權利金營收
+13.4%YOY，PUF-based 權利
金+927%YOY 
 2026 前三季權利金營收 21.49 億元，+13.4%YOY。 
成熟製程 NeoBit 佔 24.6%，+8.6%YOY。 
先進製程 NeoFuse 佔 70.7%，+12.3%YOY。 
PUF-based 佔 1.5%，+927%YOY。 
MTP 佔 3.2%，+32.5%YOY。 
權利金成長預計將持續加速，主要受惠於先進製程應用的 ASP 提升、客戶新產
品持續放量、PUF 相關部署的營收貢獻增加，以及高權利金費率應用的產品組
合比重提高。

二、2026 年前三季授權金
+38.2% YOY，PUF - based 
+234% YOY 
 2026 前三季授權金營收 12.51 億元，+38.2%YOY。 
NeoBit 佔 11.9%，-39.3%YOY。 
NeoFuse 佔 36.5%，+35.1%YOY。 
PUF-based 佔 31.7%，+234%YOY。 
MTP 佔 19.9%，+22.9%YOY。 
授權業務預期將維持強勁的成長動能，主要受惠於先進與成熟製程節點、硬體安
全、AI 基礎設施，以及次世代快閃記憶體 Flash 應用等領域。

三、維持買進理由  1) 受惠於先進製程應用的 ASP 提升、客戶新產品持續放量、PUF 相關部署的
營收貢獻增加，以及高權利金費率應用的產品組合比重提高，權利金成長預計將
持續加速。 
2) 授權業務預期將維持強勁的成長動能，主要受惠於先進與成熟製程節點、硬
體安全、AI 基礎設施，以及次世代快閃記憶體 Flash 應用等領域。 
3) PUF-based 已貢獻 2026 年前三季營收 12.6%，+252%YOY。應用面包括
CPU、Storage、光通、FPGA、高速傳輸晶片多面向。Arm Neoverse CSS V3
已將力旺的 PUF 列入核心元件。

## Document 223: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다실적에 수급을 더한다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다실적에 수급을 더한다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
여전히 가격 상승 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 95.8조원, 영업이익 75.1조원으로 예상한다. 
이전 전망 대비 감소한 수준이다. 원/달러 환율 영향이다. 영업이익률은 78.4%로 전 
분기 대비 2.1%p 상승할 전망이다. DRAM 매출액은 73.1조원, 영업이익 
59.2조원으로 추정한다. DRAM ASP는 이전 전망 대비 상향 조정했다. 일부 제품 
가격 인상을 반영한 영향이다. Bit Growth는 이전 전망 수준을 유지했다. ASP 
상향이 수익성 개선에 미친 영향은 크지 않을 전망이다. NAND 매출액은 22.4조원, 
영업이익은 15.9조원으로 예상한다. 
Global Top 수준 주주환원 규모/자사주 살 게 더 있다 
SK하이닉스는 2026년 8월 19일 주주환원에 대한 새로운 정책을 제시했다. FCF에서 
CAPEX를 제외한 금액의 50% 이상을 주주환원에 사용한다는게 핵심이다. 2026년 
기준으로 최소 90조원 수준일 것으로 추정한다. 자사주 중심으로 주주환원을 계획 
중이고 이미 집행 중인 40조원 이외에 4분기 중에 추가로 자사주 매입이 진행될 
것으로 예상한다. 배당도 일부 집행이 되겠지만 자사주가 중심이 될 전망이다. 
흔들리지 말아야 할 이유는 많다 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 모델의 성장으로 메모리에 
대한 수요가 지속적으로 높아지고 있고, 이에 비해 공급 증가는 제한적인 상황이 
상당기간 지속될 것으로 전망하기 때문이다. 이로 인해 현재 수준의 DRAM, NAND 
가격에 변동성은 낮고, 높은 수익성이 유지되면서 안정적 현금흐름 창출이 가능할 
것으로 전망한다. 현재 주가는 지나치게 저평가되어 있는 국면에 있다고 판단한다. 
목표주가는 12개월 예상 EPS 446,092원에 PER 9배를 적용한 4,000,000원을 
유지한다.

동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일

## Document 227: [005380] 현대차(005380) 3Q26 Re_ 왜 서프를 내고 주가는 하락했는가현대차(005380) 3Q26 Re_ 왜 서프를 내고 주가는 하락했는가3Q26 Re_ 왜 서프를_2c20e0c2

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005380] 현대차(005380) 3Q26 Re_ 왜 서프를 내고 주가는 하락했는가현대차(005380) 3Q26 Re_ 왜 서프를 내고 주가는 하락했는가3Q26 Re_ 왜 서프를_2c20e0c2.pdf

 절대 (16.4) (17.3) 13.4   
 상대 (3.3) (21.1) (22.1)  
 절대 (달러환산) (15.2) (10.5) 21.4

3Q26 OP 2.7조, 당사 추정치 35% 상회 
서프라이즈의 근거가 판매보증비 감소에서 발생. 3Q26 기말환율의 낙폭 (-12% qoq)으로 
인한 역설적 비용절감의 규모가 전년동기 대비 1.2조 원을 넘어서며 환율의 조사모삼 효과
로 만들어진 컨센서스 상회 실적. 
긍정적인 부분: 컨틴전시, 신차 효과, 인센티브, 생산 정상화 
지속적인 컨틴전시 체제를 유지하며 비용절감 실천 (=지속된 요소). 4분기 추가적으로 나아
질 요인은 1) 내수 신차효과로 판가 상승 및 유기적 수익성 개선, 2) 공장 가동률 정상화를 
통한 고정비 부담 절감, 3) 해외시장 신차효과를 통한 딜러 인센티브 절감. 
경계할 부분: 재료비, 환율, BEV 판매비중 확대 
재료비 측면에서 1) 2Q26 시작된 미국-이란 전쟁 관련 원재료비 상승은 4Q26F에도 하락
세를 보이지 못할 것이며, 2) 구리, 철강 등의 금속 원자재의 가격 상승 또한 4Q26F 지속될 
것. 또한 3Q26과 반대로 qoq 기말환율 대비 낙폭 확대할 평균환율 (mtd 1,338원) 효과로 
환 관련 감익 본격화, IONIQ 3의 출시는 인센티브 절감인 동시에 믹스 관점에서 부정적. 
투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 440,000원으로 소폭 상향 
목표주가 상향 근거는 컨틴전시 체제 유지를 통한 FY27F 자동차사업 부문 이익 예상치 상
향. 부정적 외생변수 (환율, 재료비)가 지속된다는 전제 하에 FY27F OPM 하락을 예상하나, 
(FY26F 5.6%, FY27F 5.2%) 향후 신차효과와 생산 정상화에 따른 자동차 사업부문 수익성 
반등의 여력 존재. 주가 상승 트리거는 1) CES 전후 BD 관련 기대감, 2) 신차 출시 이후 견
조한 판매량, 3) 환율, 원재료비 등 외생변수의 개선.

## Document 246: [034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡_62bfa2bd

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡_62bfa2bd.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30

Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

Company Brief 
유틸리티/건설 
Analyst 문경원, CFA 
kyeongwon.moon@meritz.co.kr 
RA 이동석 
dongseok.lee@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 Review: 바닥을 잡는다면 지금  
믹스 개선에 따른 이익률 개선 조금씩 관찰 중 
3Q26 연결 영업이익은 2,612억원(+90.6% YoY)을 기록하며 컨센서스(2,455억원)
를 소폭 상회했다. 밥캣(1,674억원)이 컨센서스(1,761억원)를 소폭 하회한 가운데, 
에너빌리티 부문 영업이익(+179% YoY, OPM 6.6%)이 호실적을 이끌었다. 환율 하
락에도 불구, 가스터빈, 원전 등 고수익성 프로젝트의 비중 상승이 주요 원인이다. 
3Q26 누적 수주는 10.0조원(+85.5% YoY)을 기록했다. 4Q26 가스복합 EPC 수주 
등을 감안하면 연간 가이던스(13.3조원) 달성은 무난하다. 한수원과의 협상 결렬로 
이목이 집중된 체코 두코바니 시공 수주는 2027년 인식될 확률이 높아 보인다. 주간
사가 바뀌더라도 이미 프로젝트에 참여 중인 동사를 배제하기는 쉽지 않다.

대미투자 이후, 연말~연초 수주 모멘텀에 집중 
연말 연초에 다음과 수주 모멘텀이 집중되면서, 주가 상승을 이끌 전망이다. 
1) 대형원전: 정부 자금 중심의 Westinghouse LLI(장납기 기자재) 발주가 몰린다. 
우선 미국 DOE(에너지부)가 제공한 대출을 기반으로 미국 수주가 기대된다.  폴란드 
정부 역시 최근 LLI 발주 작업을 시작했다. 한편, 한국 정부는 연말까지 100억 달러 
규모의 대미 투자 송금을 협의 중인데, 이 역시 DOC(상무부) 주도의 대형 원전 건설
을 위한 LLI 발주에 쓰일 가능성이 높다 
2) SMR: 오래 기다려왔던 Nuscale 향 수주가 시작되고 있다. 우선 29일 모듈 1기 
제작 계약을 체결했다. 현금이 부족한 Nuscale이 용기를 냈다는 건, 곧 Nuscale-
TVA 간 PPA 가 임박했음을 의미한다. 본격적인 수주는 PPA 직후 예상된다.  
3) 가스 터빈: 2026년 초 해외 수주(7기)는 2029년까지 인도 계획이다. 이후 2030
년 슬랏 예약을 완료하고 2031년 물량을 논의 중으로 보인다. 지금은 2030년 슬랏
에 대한 PO가 나와 수주잔고에 인식할 타이밍이다. 역시 수출 물량으로 추측한다.

성장에 대한 기대가 사라진 밸류에이션, 지금이 바닥 
투자의견 Buy를 유지, 할인율 상승을 반영하여 적정주가를 109,000원으로 -17% 
하향 조정한다. 금리 상승, 환율 하락, 트럼프 지지율 하락 등 Top-down 리스크에 
최근 조정을 받았지만, 추가적인 주가 하락 폭은 제한적일 전망이다. EV/선행 수주잔
고 지표는 현재 1.0배 수준까지 하락하며, 원전의 성장성이 주목받지 못했던 
2021~2024년 수준에 근접하게 내려왔다.  
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 30

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 109,000원

## Document 258: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
악조건 속에서도 이 정도 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 영업실적은 매출액 5조 7,536억원(yoy +30.2%, qoq 
`-9.1%), 영업이익 8,992억원(yoy +61.3%, qoq -13.5%)으로 당사 추정치에 대체로 
부합하였다. 조업일수 감소와 원화 강세로 매출액이 감소했으며, 임단협 타결로 
인한 성과금 및 격려금이 당분기에 비용으로 반영되었다. 해양플랜트 부문에서는 
RUYA 프로젝트의 공사 진행률 확대와 TRION 프로젝트의 C/O(계약 변경) 
180억원이 추가로 반영되며 매출액이 전분기대비 0.7% 증가하였다. 엔진기계 
부문은 환율 하락을 판가 상승과 D/F(이중연료) 비중 증가로 상쇄하며 전분기와 
유사한 영업실적을 달성했다. 
2027년에도 지속될 수주 시점 효과 
3분기 대형선 야드의 수주 시점별 매출 비중은 2022년 4%, 2023년 45%, 2024년 
31%, 2025년 20% 수준으로 파악된다. 상대적으로 선가가 낮았던 2022~2023년 
물량이 여전히 매출의 절반 가량을 차지하고 있는 셈이다. 따라서 2027년 말까지도 
고선가 물량의 매출 비중 확대가 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이는 LNG운반선 매출 
비중 감소에도 불구하고 수익성을 방어할 수 있는 핵심 요인으로 작용할 전망이다.   
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 550,000원으로 하향 
투자의견은 매수로 유지하나, 목표주가는 550,000원으로 34.5% 하향한다. 환율 
가정을 기존 1,450원 수준에서 1,400원으로 조정함에 따라 목표주가 산정의 기준이 
되는 2027~2028년 실적추정치를 하향조정하였다. 또한 미국 네오 클라우드 
업체들의 자금 조달 여건 악화로 데이터센터 증설이 지연되고 있어, 발전용 엔진 
증설을 근거로 동사에 부여하였던 프리미엄을 제거하여 P/E 배수를 19.8배로 
하향조정했다. 그러나 본업인 상선 부문은 향후 고부가가치 가스선의 발주 강세를 
전망하기 때문에 투자의견은 매수로 유지한다.

## Document 280: AI股票抛售是否过度？还是华尔街抢先下调了预期？

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI股票抛售是否过度？还是华尔街抢先下调了预期？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⾼盛数据显⽰，美国科技股多空⽐已降⾄ 2016 年以来最低， CTA 持有约 39 亿美元纳指空
头， AI 半导体板块 2028 年预期市盈率仅 11 倍，较标普 500 折价近三成，市场定价已隐含盈
利下滑约 6% 。但卖⽅仍预期板块 2028 年盈利增⻓ 31% 。这究竟意味着 AI 股票遭遇了过度
抛售，还是华尔街正在抢先下调盈利预期？  随着 AI 信⽤利差升⾄两年⾼位、融资压⼒持续
加剧，英伟达财报与 Anthropic IPO 将成为检验市场预期的关键。
摘要：
六周前，市场争论的还是 AI 仓位该持有多少；如今，问题已变成还有谁愿意接盘。AI 交易从⼈满
为患到⽆⼈问津，速度之快令⼈咋⾆，⽽这⼀切恰好发⽣在本年度 AI 主题最关键的⼀周前⼣。
据⾼盛资⾦流向策略师 Lee Coppersmith 在最新周报中指出，过去⼀个⽉，该⾏客⼾对 AI 主题的
情绪已 " 急剧转变 " ，悲观程度为近年来罕⻅，且这种悲观情绪已明显反映在仓位和估值层⾯。
与此同时，⾼盛旗下 AI 半导体篮⼦（ GSCBSMHX ）⽬前仅以 2028 年预期盈利的 11 倍交易，远

低于标普 500 的 16 倍 —— 尽管卖⽅共识预测该板块 2028 年盈利将增⻓ 31% 。
这⼀估值差异指向⼀个核⼼问题：要么此轮抛售已经过度，要么市场正在抢先定价盈利预期下
调。英伟达将于本周⼆发布财报（这也是创始⼈离任后的⾸份业绩）， Anthropic 的 IPO 则定于周
四登场，两⼤事件或将成为检验市场信⼼的关键节点。
从做多到做空：机构仓位急剧逆转
专业投资者的⾏动已远不⽌于减仓。 Coppersmith 援引⾼盛主经纪商数据显⽰，上周科技股再度
成为全球净卖出规模最⼤的板块，美国信息技术板块的多空⽐降⾄ 2016 年以来最低⽔平。更值
得关注的是，此前以平仓和降杠杆为主的资⾦流向，已转变为明显的新增做空⾏为。
与此同时，⻓线资⾦也在持续减持英伟达，转⽽流向超⼤规模云计算企业 —— ⽽后者的资本开
⽀，恰恰是英伟达的营收来源。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

系统性策略⽅⾯， CTA （商品交易顾问）同样在⼤举做空科技股。⾼盛模型显⽰，趋势跟踪基⾦
⽬前持有约 39 亿美元的纳斯达克 100 指数空头敞⼝，接近 2018 年底、 2022 年及 2025 年春季的阶
段性低点 —— ⽽上述每⼀次低点，事后均标志着⾏情的转折。
估值已隐含盈利萎缩，历史性折价引发争议
估值层⾯的数据同样引⼈注⽬。
⾃ 6 ⽉以来，费城半导体指数（ SOX ）相对标普 500 的跌幅已超过 30 个百分点，为 2002 年以来最
⼤相对回撤。截⾄上周五， SOX/SPX ⽐率较过去⼀年⾼点低 31.5% ，⽽历史最⼤跌幅为
57.3% ，出现于 2000 年 11 ⽉。
Coppersmith 指出，⾼盛研究团队认为部分个股的跌幅已经过度 —— 当前价格隐含的是 2028 年
盈利接近零增⻓的情景。若以与标普 500 相同的估值倍数衡量，当前股价实际上隐含该板块盈利
将下滑约 6% 。
历史规律表明，当卖⽅预期与市场定价出现如此剧烈的背离时，往往是盈利预期跟随股价下

调，⽽⾮股价向预期回归。价格⽬标同样倾向于跟随股价移动。
两⼤核⼼忧虑：谁来买单，能否建成
Coppersmith 将客⼾对话归结为两个核⼼问题：其⼀，谁来承担数千亿美元的 AI 追加⽀出？其
⼆，能否真正建成⾜够多的数据中⼼？
在数据中⼼建设⽅⾯，⾼盛研究团队认为市场担忧有所过度。即便在较为悲观的情景下， 2028
年新增数据中⼼容量仍将与 2027 年⼤体相当，若更多项⽬推进，规模甚⾄可能⾼出 50% 以上。
然⽽，据路透等媒体报道， Bernstein 对在建项⽬的独⽴审计显⽰，美国已公告数据中⼼容量
中，仅约四分之⼀具备可信度，这为乐观预期打上了折扣。
融资问题则被视为更根本的隐患。 Coppersmith 直⾔， AI 企业的⽀出已超过⾃⾝现⾦流，意味着
必须持续借债或引⼊外部投资者。然⽽借贷成本正在上升，投资者开始质疑巨额⽀出能否带来
回报。⾼盛投资研究部⻔认为，英伟达现有承诺或可维持，但同等规模的新融资将愈发困难。
信⽤市场的信号印证了这⼀判断：AI 相关投资级信⽤利差已扩⼤⾄约 140 个基点，为过去两年最

⾼⽔平，较⾮ AI 信⽤利差⾼出约 40 个基点。与此同时，⾼盛预计 2027 年美国信⽤债发⾏规模将
再创历史新⾼，超过 2.7 万亿美元。
英伟达财报与 Anthropic IPO ：催化剂还是顶部信号？

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
本周的两⼤事件或将为市场提供⽅向性参考。英伟达将于当地时间周⼆收盘后发布财报，该股
已连续六周下跌，累计跌幅达 30% 。
Anthropic 则定于周四上市，据报道认购订单已超额数倍，但⾄少有⼀位与 Peter Thiel 相关的投
资⼈警告，若上市表现疲软，可能引发整个 AI 交易的连锁崩塌。
即便两⼤事件均顺利过关， Coppersmith 援引⾼盛 2027 年市场展望指出， AI ⽀出增速可能于明年
初⻅顶，⽽市场对增速放缓的反应往往远⽐对绝对数字的反应更为敏感。
数据显⽰， AI 贡献了美国 GDP 增⻓的 21% ，却贡献了企业盈利增⻓的 62% ，以及标普 500 年内市
值涨幅峰值时的 92%—— 股市对 AI 的依赖程度远超实体经济。
仓位已净化，但融资才是真正考验
Coppersmith 的最终⽴场审慎⽽折中：科技股仓位已⼤幅净化，可逢弱势逐步加仓，但不宜追

涨，同时应保持对冲纪律，并关注 AI 基础设施以外的轮动机会。
仓位层⾯的极度净化 —— 科技股超配⽐例降⾄历史低位、 CTA 接近最⼤空头、半导体估值隐含盈
利萎缩 —— 确实为剧烈逼空⾏情提供了⼟壤，英伟达财报或 Anthropic 上市均可能成为导⽕索。
然⽽，仓位可以解释反弹，却⽆法为数据中⼼提供资⾦。真正的考验在信⽤市场： AI 利差处于两
年⾼位，⾼收益债买家已基本离场，⽽⾏业明年仍计划创纪录地举债融资。 Anthropic 的巨型
IPO 或许能短暂提振市场信⼼，但也可能成为最后⼀批⼤型 AI ⻛险买家悉数⼊场的时刻 —— 恰好
与⽀出增速⻅顶的时间窗⼝重合。
专业投资者已⽤空头仓位表明了⽴场。本周，市场将开始寻找答案。

Weekly Economics
U.S. Week Ahead
Existing Home Sales • Thursday
Housing market conditions remain highly unfavorable. Mortgage
rates are the primary headwind, and they have climbed even higher
alongside the rise in longer-term yields. At the same time, low
supply is preventing a broad correction in home prices, leaving
prospective buyers squeezed on two fronts.
We expect that existing home sales slid 3.4% in August to an
annualized rate of 3.92 million. Behind this forecast is ongoing

weakness in mortgage applications and a recent bounce in
mortgage rates, which rose from 6.43% at the beginning of July to
6.66% by month-end.
We do not expect affordability conditions to meaningfully improve
in the near term. Supply is likely to remain constrained while high
mortgage rates discourage mobility. And mortgage rates are apt
to remain elevated amid firm economic growth expectations and
heavy government and corporate debt issuance.

### Page 3

Economics Week Ahead Economics
CPI • Friday
Headline inflation looks to have picked up last month. We estimate
the CPI rose 0.40% in August, with gasoline prices increasing a little
over 4% as renewed tensions in the Middle East pushed oil higher.
Grocery prices also likely rebounded after modestly declining in July.
Outside of food and energy, price pressures appear little changed.
We expect the core CPI to rise 0.23%, essentially matching July's

pace. Core goods inflation should remain firm, led by another solid
increase in used vehicle prices as consumer prices continue to
catch up to the trend indicated at wholesale auctions. Meantime,
household furnishings and other tariff-sensitive categories appear
to be stabilizing after declining earlier this year.
Core services inflation should be little changed with another 0.2%
monthly increase. But underneath the surface, we expect a modest
pickup in travel (led by lodging away from home) to offset slightly

cooler medical care and housing cost growth.
All told, we expect the report to show that while headline inflation
remains influenced by the ebbs and flows of the conflict in the
Middle East, core inflation remains contained. Should our forecast
prove correct, core CPI will ease to 2.4% year-over-year, in line with
its 6-month annualized rate but above the more recent 3-month
annualized pace of 1.7%.

### Page 4

Weekly Economics
G10 Week Ahead
European Central Bank Monetary Policy Meeting •
Thursday
We expect the European Central Bank (ECB) to raise the Deposit
Rate by 25 bps to 2.50% next week. August headline inflation
remained elevated at 3.3% year-over-year, though softer core
and services inflation suggest underlying pressures continue to
moderate. Still, the more hawkish tone of the July minutes and
renewed rise in energy prices should keep policymakers cautious.

The recent escalation in the Middle East has pushed fuel prices
at the pump close to their 2022 peaks, while natural gas storage
remains below historical levels, leaving the Eurozone vulnerable
to further energy price increases. The ECB is unlikely to respond
aggressively if the shock remains confined to energy given the
downside risks to growth. However, a broader rise in food inflation
linked to El Niño would be more concerning, as highly visible prices

could lift inflation expectations and generate second-round effects.
For now, resilient Q3 activity, elevated headline inflation and
contained wage growth support one final hike. The ECB will likely
retain and emphasize its scenario-based outlook in the updated
staff projections given continued uncertainty around the conflict
and energy prices. The policy path beyond September will depend
on whether energy and food pressures persist and begin to spread,

which could leave room for further tightening.

U.K. Monthly GDP • Friday
We expect UK GDP to rise 0.2% month-over-month in July, with
growth of 0.3% on a three-month-over-three-month basis.

Activity likely moderated after June’s strong rebound, though
services remained supportive as the July services PMI rose to 52.1.
Retail sales volumes fell 0.5% month-over-month, while softer
manufacturing activity likely reflected some reversal of earlier
precautionary stockbuilding.
The moderation appears more consistent with payback from
June than a broader deterioration in momentum. August PMIs
remain consistent with Q3 GDP growth of around 0.3% quarter-

over-quarter. Still, the renewed escalation in the Middle East and
associated rise in gas and energy prices adds upside risk to inflation
and could weigh on household demand. Against this backdrop and
with Bank of England hawks continuing to emphasize the need for
tighter policy, we expect the BoE to remain cautious and continue
to look for a rate hike in Q4. -1.0%

### Page 5

Economics Week Ahead Economics
EM Week Ahead
Mexico CPI • Wednesday
We expect Mexico’s August inflation rate to edge higher, with
headline CPI rising to 3.34% year-over-year (0.27% month-over-
month) from 3.26% year-over-year in early August. Part of the
pickup likely reflects less favorable base effects and a smaller
disinflationary contribution from food prices. While agricultural
prices continued to help restrain inflation in mid-August, the drag

was notably less pronounced than in July. Core inflation should
remain elevated at 3.98% year-over-year (0.25% month-over-
month).
While inflation remains within Banxico’s midpoint 3% target,
persistent core and services inflation suggest convergence toward
the midpoint of the target could prove gradual. For now, we
continue to expect Banxico to keep rates on hold, with the policy
rate remaining at 6.50% through the rest of 2026 and into early
2027 as policymakers await clearer evidence that underlying

inflation pressures are easing sustainably. While rate cuts remain
a possibility in 2027, the current inflation backdrop argues for
patience in the near term.

Brazil CPI • Friday
We expect Brazil’s August inflation rate to slow to 4.20% year-over-
year, helped by lower electricity, food and fuel prices. The main
driver should be a temporary reduction in residential electricity bills
stemming from a one-off credit, while recent data already point to
a notable easing in price pressures. Mid-August CPI came in weaker
than expected, with inflation falling to 4.24% year-over-year amid
declines in electricity, food, fuel and airfare prices. Beyond these

temporary factors, restrictive monetary policy continues to weigh
on economic activity, as evidenced by the recent slowdown in Q2
GDP growth. However, underlying inflation risks remain. A still-
tight labor market and government measures aimed at supporting
consumption could keep services inflation sticky, making progress
toward the 3.0% inflation target more gradual.
Overall, the inflation outlook remains consistent with additional
policy easing. We expect COPOM to deliver a 25 bps rate cut in

September, bringing the Selic Rate to 13.75%. Beyond September,
policymakers are likely to proceed cautiously as they assess
incoming inflation data and the impact of the election cycle on
inflation expectations. Our base case remains for a pause after
September, with additional easing more likely in 2027.

## Document 346: FX Daily_ Don’t mess with the dollar near term

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ Don’t mess with the dollar near term.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5

### Page 1

Hot US inﬂation and the prospect of a more aggressive
response from the Fed are the themes dominating FX markets
currently. Another dose of ﬁrm US PPI today and some more
hawkish Fed rhetoric are set to keep the dollar very supported
into next week

Chris Turner, Frantisek Taborsky and Francesco Pesole
October US PPI data today should keep short-dated yields and the dollar bid
Please see ING's FX Outlook 2027: Fat tails, slim ranges for
insights into key themes next year.
USD: Do not stand in the way of the
dollar
This week has seen a powerful, macro-driven rally in the dollar.
Short-dated US yields are close to 40bp higher since Tuesday
morning, when the Federal Reserve's Christopher Waller mused
over the need for a 50bp hike in December. The market has since

### Page 2

adopted the view that back-to-back December and January hikes
look likely, while extending the full Fed adjustment from here to a
full 85bp of tightening. That's beginning to look a lot more like a
cycle than the kind of modest adjustment we recently presented in
our house baseline view. Notably, a few Fed members are starting
to describe conditions more as a demand than a supply shock.
It is hard to see what is going to change this hawkish Fed/dollar-

positive mindset in the next few weeks. The Fed centrists might try
to emphasise the need for a gradual adjustment in policy, but it is
probably only a big US tech sell-oﬀ that can put a dent in short-
dated US yields now.
In terms of the FX reaction, the low-yielding Swiss franc and the yen
have some of the highest correlations with US rates and are under
pressure. 0.8400 is a really important resistance area for USD/CHF
and will probably help determine whether the dollar needs to take

another big leg higher over the coming weeks. USD/JPY is already
trading through the psychological level at 160, and we would not
expect any intervention yet given the macro nature of the broadly
stronger dollar. As we said on Wednesday, we expect to see dollar
strength more expressed against activity currencies, where
something like the low-yielding Swedish krona looks vulnerable.
For today, the October US PPI looks likely to keep the narrative of

hot US inﬂation alive. Expect scrutiny into the components of PPI,
such as healthcare, airfares and software, that feed into the Fed's
preferred PCE inﬂation data – the next release of which is on 25
November. Were that core PCE to rise 0.3% month-on-month for the
third month in a row, expectations of a 50bp December hike would
grow.
DXY has broken to a new high for the year and looks like it can push
on to the next resistance level at 104.60. That's about a 1.5%

advance from current levels and would be consistent with EUR/USD
trading around 1.0880.
Chris Turner
EUR: ECB struggles to outhawk the Fed

### Page 3

European Central Bank rhetoric ﬁrmly points to the need for a rate
hike in December. The problem for EUR/USD is that the market sees
the potential for a prolonged tightening cycle as more credible in
the US than in the eurozone. You don't hear many ECB speakers
talking of a demand shock in Europe. Short-dated EUR/USD swap
diﬀerentials have moved out to the widest levels of the year and are
clearly weighing on the pair.
Given the run of expected US inﬂation data over the coming weeks,

we struggle to see what can take the pressure oﬀ EUR/USD. A break
below 1.10 is not a given, but that level is certainly at risk into year-
end. EUR/USD bulls will have to be hoping for either an AI crash, the
ECB sounding far more hawkish or perhaps some major
geopolitical breakthrough in Iran or Ukraine.
In focus today will be the second release of third-quarter eurozone
GDP data. This printed a respectable 0.4% quarter-on-quarter
previously. And there are also several ECB speakers – all likely to

point to a December rate hike.
Chris Turner
CEE: The pressure on FX is far from over
Friday will bring more data to the CEE region than the relatively
quiet days we have seen this week. This morning, Q3 GDP ﬁgures
for Romania and Poland will be released. While the former is
expected to show some acceleration, the latter is likely to reﬂect a
slowdown. Poland will also release its ﬁnal inﬂation reading for
October, expected at 3.5%, and on Monday, we'll receive the core

inﬂation ﬁgure, which we estimate has slowed to 2.8%.
In Turkey, inﬂation expectations for November will be published.
Yesterday, the Central Bank of Turkey revised its inﬂation forecast
for next year from 15% to 21%, bringing it closer to the current
market expectation of 23% but also opening the door to a
December rate cut. The Czech National Bank will release the
minutes from last week’s meeting, during which it raised interest
rates by 25bp to 4.00%. However, the 4-3 vote split sent a dovish

The CEE market remains under pressure. Yesterday’s surge in

take a ﬁrm stance next week and avoid rushing into rate cuts
despite low inﬂation, which could support the forint, given that one
to two rate cuts are currently priced in.

more dovish tone than what we observed last week.
Frantisek Taborsky
BRL: Lula trying to take some ﬁscal
action
The Brazilian real has slumped since incumbent President Luiz

Inácio Lula da Silva beat Flavio Bolsonaro in the run-oﬀ vote last
month. At the heart of the concern is the ﬁscal side, where Lula's
pre-election proposals have only added to concern about Brazil's
ﬁscal position. Over the last few days, however, Lula's government
has started to propose some ﬁscal consolidation for the 2027
budget, and we presume more is coming.
While Brazil's ﬁscal story is vulnerable, its monetary story is
supportive for the real. High real interest rates and an independent

central bank make the Brazilian real an expensive sell. We look for a
recovery in 2027. Please see the Latam section in our 2027 FX
Outlook for more.
Chris Turner

## Document 347: FX Daily_ Time for a break below 1.10 in EUR_USD_

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ Time for a break below 1.10 in EUR_USD_.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

EUR/USD is ready to test the key 1.100 support. We think the
pair can hold the line in a light week for US data, but risks of a
break lower are meaningful given the macro nature of this USD
rally. Any AI-related equity market instability probably has a
greater chance of denting this USD momentum than Fedspeak
this week

Francesco Pesole, Frantisek Taborsky and Chris Turner
EUR/USD could test 1.10 if upcoming data provides further support for the
dollar
Please see ING's FX Outlook 2027: Fat tails, slim ranges for
insights into key themes next year.
USD: Strong momentum looks harder to
unwind
The macro backdrop makes it risky to call for a dollar correction just
yet. Two-year USD swap rates have risen 35bp since the Federal

### Page 2

Reserve's Christopher Waller mentioned a 50bp hike and core CPI
overshot on 10 November, while other Fed speakers have done little
to quell the hawkish repricing. We’d be more bearish on the dollar if
markets had jumped to pricing a 50bp move in December, which
seems unlikely. Instead, the Fed funds futures curve favours back-
to-back 25bp increases in December and January. That looks not
only more realistic but, crucially, considerably harder for moderate

FOMC members to push back against than an imminent 50bp
move.
We therefore do not see this week’s long list of Fed speakers as a
signiﬁcant downside risk for the dollar. Chair Kevin Warsh speaks
today, but he’s been wary of oﬀering guidance, especially outside
of meetings, so we wouldn't be surprised to get very little from his
remarks. The minutes from the October FOMC meeting are due on
Wednesday. The recent hawkish turn in Fedspeak and hotter core

CPI have probably raised the bar for a market-impactful surprise.
The larger risk of a USD correction comes from US equities, which
have shown great potential to drive dovish repricing. The AI trade
faces two major tests this week: Nvidia reports quarterly earnings
tomorrow, while Anthropic is expected to make its stock market
debut on Thursday. So far, tech stocks have held up remarkably
well despite Nvidia’s idiosyncratic selloﬀ, and futures currently point

to a modestly positive start to the week.
Today’s Empire Manufacturing index is one of the few notable data
inputs this week. Given the size of the front-end rates move and the
arguably high bar for further hawkish surprises, we tend to favour
dollar stabilisation rather than another leg higher. But for now, we
think markets will tend to fade dollar corrections.
Francesco Pesole
EUR: 1.100 test coming
With the exception of tomorrow’s ZEW release, the eurozone macro

calendar is mostly centred on central bank speakers this week,
including outgoing ECB President Christine Lagarde on Thursday
and Friday. While the European Central Bank's rhetoric has

### Page 3

remained broadly hawkish-leaning, the contrast with Fed members’
comments is stark.
That divergence is mirrored in the EUR/USD 2-year swap rate
diﬀerential, which has widened by nearly 35bp to its widest since
September 2025. EUR/USD was some 5-6% higher back then, but
the backdrop couldn’t be more diﬀerent. The dollar was still
suﬀering from a material post-Liberation Day risk premium. Now, it
is the euro facing some premium due to heightened eurozone ﬁscal

risk. More importantly, the real front-end rate diﬀerential is
signiﬁcantly lower. Adjusted for 2yr inﬂation swaps, it is at its
widest since December 2022.
Our macro team still thinks markets are overestimating US inﬂation
risks and will face large dovish repricing, but that doesn’t seem a
very near-term story. Given the lighter US calendar, we feel EUR/USD
can hold the line at 1.100 this week, but risks of a break lower are
signiﬁcant, especially as that would be only a small stretch relative

to short-term fundamentals. For now, EUR/USD optimists like us
should be content with some stabilisation in the 1.10-1.11 range.
Francesco Pesole
CAD: Inﬂation shouldn't endorse a
December hike
Canadian inﬂation data for October is released today. Headline CPI
should accelerate by 0.3% month-on-month, as in the US, taking
the yearly rate slightly higher from 3.1% to 3.2%. That won’t
matter too much unless the so far extra-stable core measures start

to climb. Expectations are for only a small 0.1ppt tick higher in
median and trim CPI, with both staying anchored around 2.0%.
Don’t expect the Bank of Canada to turn particularly more hawkish
on this inﬂation backdrop. December remains a risk for a surprise
hike (15bp priced in), but we think January is more likely.
The widening rate diﬀerential has pushed USD/CAD close to the
October highs (1.430 intraday). That is despite the Canadian dollar
outperforming all other G10 currencies over the past week, in line

with its lower negative correlation with front-end USD swings. While

### Page 4

FX
not our base case, a break of 1.430 would technically pave the way
for much more upside, with 1.450 the next key resistance.
Francesco Pesole
CEE: Vain wait for sustainable relief
The second half of the month is typically quieter in the CEE region.
Today, Poland’s core inﬂation for October will be released; we
estimate it has fallen to 2.8%. In the Czech Republic, October PPI
ﬁgures will be published – a metric that has consistently surprised
on the upside in recent months.

Tomorrow, the National Bank of Hungary decides on interest rates;
we expect no change, with the rate remaining at 5.50%. Inﬂation
has rebounded, and we anticipate further upside. Although the
headline ﬁgure should remain near the inﬂation target, global
uncertainty will likely lead the central bank to delay rate cuts and
focus instead on maintaining a stable exchange rate. Throughout
the remainder of the week, we will see more secondary data, such

as wage ﬁgures in Hungary and industrial production in Poland.
On the global front, we have seen little change in recent days. If
anything, the situation for the CEE region has deteriorated further.
The US dollar is testing new highs, and global energy prices have
seen a rebound. Meanwhile, central banks in the region remain on
the dovish side. Overall, this leaves our view – which has been
bearish on CEE FX since the end of the summer – essentially

unchanged looking forward.
EUR/CZK has likely absorbed all the dovishness from the Czech
National Bank's last meeting. We believe the repricing is complete,
and current levels around 24.400 appear fair. On the other hand,
EUR/PLN and EUR/HUF likely have more upside. However, the forint
could receive some support from the NBH tomorrow, if our hawkish
view that the central bank will not rush into rate cuts proves correct.
Frantisek Taborsky

## Document 420: Tracking Europe’s AI transition_ A new STOXX 600 earnings-call framework (2026-11-11)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Tracking Europe’s AI transition_ A new STOXX 600 earnings-call framework (2026-11-11).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

Tracking Europe’s AI transition:  
A new STOXX 600 earnings-call 
framework

1 
Authors 
Michael Kirker 
Economist

November 11, 2026 
How quickly is AI moving from experimentation into everyday business, and 
where are companies reporting tangible gains? The answers matter for 
institutional investors and policymakers assessing productivity, operating 
efficiency and labour demand. Yet comparable evidence across Europe remains 
limited, making it difficult to track the pace and scale of the transition. 1 
We address this gap with a proprietary framework that turns what is said on 
STOXX 600 earnings call into three structured indicators: AI adoption 
intensity, reported productivity benefits, and the impact on workforces. 
Large language models analyse what management teams say about AI’s 
operational role, applying a common scoring framework across companies, 
sectors and countries. Our new approach provides a timely pan-European 
comparable tracker that captures not just whether AI is being mentioned, but its 
real-world impact as the adoption of the new technology unfolds. 
Key Takeaways: 
— Adoption is broadening, with signs of faster progress. AI-use scores rose 
gradually from a low base before strengthening over the past 12 months. The 
increase extends across sectors, although the pace and level of adoption 
remain uneven. 
— Workforce impacts remain limited in corporate disclosures. The 
framework does not show a broad negative workforce signal attributable to 
AI. The aggregate score remains close to the level of no impact on the labour 
force across all of our sample. However, this does not rule out AI displacing 
workers at a future date. 
— Geographic differences persist within the listed-company sample. Since 
2022, German companies in the STOXX 600 have generally recorded above-
average AI-use and reported productivity scores. Dutch and Spanish 
companies in the STOXX 600 have tended to score lower on AI use, although 
Spain has recently gone through a rapid catch up. 
— The technology J-Curve: The findings are consistent with an early stage of 
the AI transition. Companies report increasing adoption of the new 
technology, but transformational productivity gains and extensive workforce 
restructuring remain uncommon.

1 1 Our US team regularly publish on data from the USCB showing the impact of 
AI on businesses, and our German team have recently begun their own 
chartbook. In the UK, the Office for National Statistics publishes on the topic too.

### Page 2

Deutsche Bank AG Page 2

Methodology 
Sample period: Our sample begins in January 2022, providing a baseline before 
ChatGPT’s launch on 30 November 2022. AI in this framework covers both 
established and emerging technologies, so the launch of ChatGPT is a reference 
point rather than the start of all corporate AI adoption. 
Timing of observations: STOXX 600 companies report on different financial 
calendars and at quarterly or semi-annual intervals. Their earnings calls also mix 
discussion of completed activity with forward-looking guidance. 
We assign each transcript to the calendar quarter in which the earnings call takes 
place, regardless of the financial reporting period discussed. The resulting 
indicators therefore track when information about AI use and its operational 
impacts becomes public rather than when implementation or the reported effect 
occurred. This provides a common dating convention across the sample while 
retaining differences in reporting frequency and disclosure timing.

Figure 1: Coverage of the STOXX 600 earnings calls

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: Share of the STOXX 600 is based on the current company weight in the index. Data collection for the current quarter (Q4 2026) is incomplete 
as some companies are still to report

Constructing the STOXX 600 AI corpus: We use a specialised AI transcription 
engine to process earnings calls from STOXX 600 companies, restricting the 
analysis to calls for which we obtain complete transcripts of sufficient accuracy. 
In Q3 2026, the latest full quarter, the dataset covered more than 500 companies, 
representing approximately 90% of the index’s current market capitalisation 
weights (Figure 1). In the current quarter (Q4) earnings calls are still taking place. 
Nearly 250 companies with an equivalent weight to around c.60% of the STOXX 
index having already reported. 
Historical coverage typically ranges from 300 to 500 companies and represents 
more than 80% of the index’s current market-capitalisation weights. Coverage 
peaks twice a year because some European companies report semi-annually.  
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 3

Deutsche Bank AG Page 3

### Page 4

Deutsche Bank AG Page 4

For examples of the AI’s scoring and justifications, see the tables presented in the 
Annex at the end of this report. 
Summary statistics 
Figure 2 shows the distribution of scores across all earnings-call transcripts in the 
historical sample, without weighting for company size or reporting frequency. 
These are transcript-level distributions rather than shares of companies, and they 
combine early and recent observations. They provide context for the quarterly 
indicators: 
• AI use score: Approximately 60% of earnings call transcripts do not 
discuss AI use. Among the minority that do discuss it, most fall into the 
“Active implementation” range (a score of 4-6 out of 10). 
• AI’s productivity impact: Nearly 70% of earnings calls do not mention a 
tangible AI-related productivity benefit. Among calls that do, scores are 
generally in the medium-impact band, indicating notable operational 
improvements. Transformational gains, scored at 8 or above out of 10, 
are rarely reported. 
• AI’s labour impact: Only around 10% of earnings calls discuss AI-related 
workforce adjustments. Reported changes typically receive scores of 1–
2 in magnitude on the −5 to +5 scale, with a slight tilt towards hiring and 
upskilling rather than redundancies.

Figure 2: Raw distribution of scores across the entire sample

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Note: Results are based on the raw number of transcripts, and are independent of firm size, and biased towards firms reporting more often.

Taken together, the distributions are consistent with an early adoption phase: 
companies discuss AI implementation more often than transformational 
productivity gains or extensive workforce restructuring. A technology J-curve 
offers one interpretation of this pattern, with investment and organisational 
adaptation preceding broader benefits.  
This J-curve pattern is strongly corroborated by external micro-data, such as the 
Banco de España’s business activity survey (EBAE) of Spanish firms, which 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 5

Deutsche Bank AG Page 5

similarly indicates that AI has a more immediate impact on upfront investment 
and productivity than on headcount. 
AI use: A broad-based rise, with signs of faster recent adoption 
The new AI-use indicator points to a broadening transition across STOXX 600 
companies, with early signs that adoption has accelerated recently. Scores rose 
only gradually from 2022 and 2024, but the latest observations suggest a 
stronger pace across industries and country groups.  
The rise is broad-based rather than confined to one sector. Technology has led 
much of the sample, while financials show tentative recent outperformance and 
utilities have caught up from a low starting point. Among the large country groups, 
German companies have persistently recorded above-average scores.  
AI use across European industries 
To trace sectoral dispersion, we group companies by STOXX industry 
classification and calculate a weighted average of their scores for each calendar 
quarter, using their latest STOXX 600 index weights. 2  
We focus on four sectors spanning different business models: financials, the 
largest services sector; industrials, the largest physical-assets sector; technology, 
a likely early adopter; and utilities, an infrastructure-heavy sector where adoption 
may proceed more slowly.  
Figure 3 presents the AI use score for these four key industries alongside the 
range for all industry scores (represented by the shaded area). 
• The AI use score is generally low over time but is gradually rising from 
around 1 point in 2022 to around 3 in 2026. 
• AI-use scores are rising across industries, but differences between 
sectors are widening. The common direction therefore points to 
broadening adoption rather than uniform progress: sectors can 
participate in the transition while remaining at different levels of use. 
In terms of the industries of interest: 
• Technology’s early lead has narrowed. The sector’s AI-use score was 
near the top of the industry range through to 2024 but has recently 
plateaued as the broader STOXX 600 average caught up during 2025–
26.3 
• Financials and industrials broadly track the index average. Both sectors 
have remained close to the aggregate AI-use score over history, with 
financials generally a little below average, and industrials a little above. 
But financials show tentative signs of moving above average in recent 
quarters.

2 The industries are: (1) Basic Materials, (2), Consumer Discretionary, (3), Consumer Staples, (4) Energy, (5) 
Financials, (6) Health Care, (7) Industrials, (9) Real Estate, (10) Technology, (11) Telecommunications, (12) 
Utilities. 
3 Q4 2025 saw a large increase in the AI use score for the “Technology” industry, jumping up to around 6. This 
one-off spike was driven by ASML’s announcement of a significant strategic partnership with and investment 
in Mistral AI” to 'embed AI across our entire holistic portfolio in order to increase the performance and 
productivity of our systems and the yield of our customers' processes'. Because of ASML’s large market cap 
(around 49% of our Technology category), it distorts the weighted average significantly. 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 6

Deutsche Bank AG Page 6

• Utilities have caught up from a low base. After recording lower AI-use 
scores than other sectors for much of the sample, utilities converged 
towards the STOXX 600 average during 2026. The result shows that the 
rise in reported adoption extends beyond the sectors most closely 
associated with AI.

Figure 3: AI use score (0-10) by industry

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The value for each industry is computed as a weighted average of all firms within the industry where the weights are derived from the current 
STOXX 600 share of each company. The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

As a cross-check, our findings align with the European Commission’s October 
Eurobarometer Flash survey of MSEs and Mid-Caps and the Eurostat annual ICT 
surveys, which show low overall intensity but a similar expansion in service- and 
tech-related sectors.  
AI use across countries 
Companies in the STOXX 600 tend to be large multi-nationals. That makes it 
difficult to pin down where physically the AI transformation is taking place within 
their organisation. Instead, our country comparison is done using two 
classifications of a company’s country: 
• Country of domicile: AI is a transformation technology, and this type of 
change is likely to be driven in organisation from the top down. We 
therefore consider a company’s country as that in which it is domiciled 
(which is likely to be where the headquarters is also). 
• Country of primary listing: While companies can generally list in other 
countries, there tends to be a home bias in their choice of exchange.  
Figure 4 presents the AI use intensity metric by both of these measures of country 
(the company’s domicile and primary exchange listing). Again, the shaded area 
shows the range across all classifications, with the largest countries/exchanges 
singled out. 
German-domiciled companies and the Xetra-listed (Frankfurt stock exchange) 
group record above-average AI-use scores in most quarters since 2022.  
Spain’s AI-use score has risen noticeably over recent quarters and now exceeds 
the aggregate average, reversing its earlier below-average position.  
11 November 2026
Focus Europe

### Page 7

Deutsche Bank AG Page 7

AI-use scores rise across country groups, with indications of faster growth from 
2025. The shared direction suggests a broad European listed-company trend, 
even though differences in score levels and timing persist between countries.

Figure 4: AI use intensity (0-10) by country

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

As a geographical cross-check, our index matches the broad trends of the 
Eurostat ICT annual survey, which places Germany as a clear frontrunner among 
its large peers. But some differences do exist. For instance, Eurostat shows the 
Netherlands with a very high AI adoption. As Figure 5 demonstrates, there is a 
positive correlation between the AI use score metric derived here, and that from 
Eurostat, even though the Eurostat data measures if firms are adopting AI, and 
our new use measure also incorporates the concept of how intensively firms are 
using AI.

11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 8

Deutsche Bank AG Page 8

Figure 5: Relationship between Eurostat usage and Weighted share of STOXX 
600 mentioning AI usage

Source: Deutsche Bank Research, Eurostat. Notes: each dot represents a country-year pair of AI usage>0

AI productivity impact: Reported benefits are rising, but 
transformational gains remain uncommon 
Higher intensity of use does not necessarily mean firms are benefiting more from 
AI. But our AI productivity impact metric does show a similar pattern to our AI 
usage metric, although it tends to be slightly lower, indicating that companies 
may not yet be reaping the full rewards for their AI investment. 
Financials stand out more clearly on reported productivity benefits than on AI-use 
intensity, while German companies again record relatively strong scores, but only 
in more recent history.

Figure 6: AI's impact on productivity (0-10) by industry

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

AI productivity impact across European industries 
Reported AI-productivity benefits have become more prominent across 
industries. Figure 6 shows scores rising gradually during 2022–25, followed by 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 9

Deutsche Bank AG Page 9

indications of a faster increase from late 2025. The profile resembles the AI-use 
trend in Figure 4, suggesting that the stronger adoption narrative is accompanied 
by more discussion of operational gains. 
The aggregate trajectories of the AI use and AI productivity impact scores are 
similar, but sector rankings differ. Technology’s early relative strength in AI use is 
not matched by its productivity score, while financials report above-average 
productivity benefits despite more modest use scores. 
AI productivity impact across countries 
Figure 7 plots the self-report impact of AI on company productivity by country. 
Between 2022-23, German companies recorded average productivity impact, 
even though their AI use score was relatively high. However more recently, their 
AI impact score has persistently been above average. The UK grouping shows 
persistent early outperformance in AI impact during 2022–23, followed by 
convergence towards the aggregate average.

Figure 7: AI's impact on productivity (0-10) by country

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

Figure 8 shows that there is a strong positive correlation between the AI use score 
and the AI productivity score across the STOXX 600 (r-squared = 0.83). The 
relationship between the scores is approximately linear, with a slope of around

0.66: a one-point higher use score is associated with roughly a 0.66-point higher 
productivity score for each country-year.

AI labour force impact: On net, AI may be benefiting 
employment, for now… 
Across industries and countries, the recorded score for AI’s impact on the labour 
force remains relatively small. On average, the reported impact on companies’ 
employment has been fairly neutral, with a marginal negative bias over the last 
year or so.

Figure 9: AI's impact on the labour force (-5 to +5) by industry

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter. Negative (positive) scores are associated with job losses (gains) 
associated with a company's AI use.

11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 11

Deutsche Bank AG Page 11

AI labour force impact across European industries 
Despite rising productivity, AI has not triggered the negative employment shock 
feared by the public. Aggregate labour impact scores across nearly all sectors 
hover near zero, with a slightly positive balance over history, and a slightly 
negative balance more recently in 2025-26 (Figure 9). But overall the magnitude 
of AI’s impact on labour remains low. For the STOXX 600 aggregate average, the 
score’s maximum deviation from 0 (no impact) is 0.43 on our 0-5 scale. 
AI labour force impact across countries 
Workforce-impact scores are also small across the main country groupings, with 
most countries slowing a slight negative tilt in recent years (Figure 10).

Figure 10: AI impact on the labour force (-5 to +5) by country

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter. Negative (positive) scores are associated with job losses (gains) 
associated with a company's AI use.

The lack of significant impact so far from AI on companies’ labour forces so far is 
consistent with results from our previous work looking at the other side of the 
labour market. Data from our dbDataInsights household survey showed that 
workers across Europe reported a fairly low level of fear about losing their job to 
AI over the next year. The analysis in that report found results that were more 
consistent with workers adapting to the new AI world by upskilling and 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 12

Deutsche Bank AG Page 12

demanding higher wages. Those results would not be consistent with a world in 
which AI was actively replacing labour. 
These findings of a low impact on labour are highly consistent with recent 
academic research (e.g., Yotzov et al., 2026), which found that nearly half of 
surveyed UK and German firms reported absolutely no impact on headcount from 
AI over the past three years, 
Conclusion 
The newly developed framework provides a recurring way to monitor company 
disclosures about AI’s impact on productivity and labour. The central finding of 
this report is a broadening AI transition rather than clear evidence of an 
economy-wide productivity breakthrough. The new metrics indicate that AI use 
remains limited but is rising across the European countries and industries 
represented in the sample. Companies report low but growing productivity gains 
and little aggregate workforce impact so far. This pattern is consistent with a 
technology J-curve, with firms still integrating the new technology, rather than 
proof that this trajectory will unfold. Limited reported workforce effects today do 
not rule out subsequent layoffs or wider organisational restructuring. 
Quarterly updates to this framework will track Europe’s AI transition and provide 
timely signals of whether companies are reporting more substantial productivity 
and employment effects. 
Beyond AI, the framework could be adapted to extract signals about employment 
decisions, investment decisions and the general economic outlook from earnings 
calls. These potential extensions could form the basis of alternative nowcasting 
models for important macroeconomic data; they are applications that will be 
explored in future work.

11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

## Document 428: US MARKETS CALL_ Below The Calm Deep Blue Sea

Source PDF: all report pdf/US MARKETS CALL_ Below The Calm Deep Blue Sea.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-14
Pages: 13
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: US MARKETS CALL: Below The Calm Deep Blue Sea
Date:    2 hours ago
US MARKETS CALL: Below The Calm Deep Blue Sea

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
US MARKETS CALL: Below The
Calm Deep Blue Sea
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 14 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Thomas Vimare / Unsplash

The major stock market indexes have gone nowhere since early August.
On the surface, the seas have been calm. Underneath, plenty of
turbulence has been occurring. Most of the volatility has been within
sectors.
In mid-September, we warned our readers to proceed with caution through
the midterm elections. We expected and are now seeing buying

### Page 2

opportunities. The S&P 500 closed at 7,561.67 on Friday. It has been
fluctuating around 7,600 since August 3. We think it is set for another rally
that should take it up to 7,900 by the end of this year.
I. Stock Market Performance
The S&P 500 is down just 0.5% since August 3, while the equal-weight
version of the index is up 1.8% (chart). The MAGS ETF is up 0.4%, while
the XMAGS ETF is up 1.2%. The Nasdaq is down 3.0%. Only the Russell
2000 has moved much, falling 6.4%.

The sectors underneath the S&P 500 have been choppier (chart). Health
Care has gained 16.4% since August 3, and Consumer Staples is up
10.8%. Communication Services is down 11.3%, and Industrials is down
7.9%. Information Technology, at the center of the AI selloff, is down 3.5%.

### Page 3

The Information Technology sector has also been extremely turbulent
below the surface (chart). The Semiconductor Equipment and
Semiconductors industries are down 23.9% and 13.6% since August 3.
Meanwhile, IT Consulting is up 22.3%. Application Software and Systems
Software are up 12.0% and 4.5%. The crosscurrents have largely offset
each other.
The Magnificent-7 stocks have diverged too (chart). Nvidia’s market cap
has dropped to $3.92 trillion following news of CEO Jensen Huang’s

resignation. Apple’s market cap is at a record $5.18 trillion. Alphabet’s fell
after reporting earnings but has since stabilized at $3.62 trillion.
II. Stock Market Earnings

### Page 4

Industry analysts are usually too optimistic about the earnings of the
companies they follow. As quarterly earnings reporting seasons approach,
they are often forced to cut their estimates. That's evident in their annual
consensus "Earnings Squiggles," which tend to start high and then fall to
the reported results (chart). This time, the annual Earnings Squiggles for
2026, 2027, and 2028 all are rising. That’s highly unusual. It might reflect
some irrational exuberance among analysts. Then again, they've gotten

more optimistic as actual earnings have come in stronger than anticipated
in recent quarters.
The recent market turbulence hasn’t dented S&P 500 operating earnings-
per-share estimates. The 2027 consensus rose to $427.01 last week, and
the 2028 consensus rose to $496.24 (chart). Both are new highs. Forward
earnings hit another record high at $420.15 per share, edging closer to the
2027 estimate. We are estimating $425 for next year.

### Page 5

Q3’s consensus earnings growth estimate rose again, to 26.9% y/y from
25.9% a week earlier (chart). Q4’s estimate edged up to 29.8%. The Q2
actual earnings growth soared on mark-to-market accounting gains.
Without them, earnings rose about 25% rather than 50.4%.
III. Stock Market Valuation
Valuation multiples continue to fall. The S&P 500’s forward P/E is down to
18.0, while the Magnificent-7’s is down to 21.4 (chart). We think the
contraction may be close to a bottom. Our year-end S&P 500 target

implies a forward P/E of 18.6. Multiples usually shrink on recession fears.
We don’t see a recession coming. Expectations of a slowdown in the AI
buildout may be weighing on valuations. In our view, the slowdown
constitutes speed bumps, not roadblocks.

### Page 6

Only 36.1% of S&P 500 stocks now trade above a forward P/E of 20,
down from nearly 50% earlier this year (chart). That’s a healthy
development, driven by the air coming out of the AI bubble, which is now
less likely to burst.
The multiple correction has been centered in S&P 500 Growth, not Value,
stocks. The S&P 500 Growth forward P/E has dropped 27% to 19.0 from
26.0 at the start of this year, while Value is at 18.0, about the same as at
the beginning of the year (chart).

### Page 7

The spread between the Growth and Value forward P/Es has narrowed to
just 1.0 point, the narrowest since 2010 (chart). Investors aren’t paying up
for growth anymore.
Only three sectors have gotten more expensive this year: Real Estate,
Consumer Staples, and Health Care (chart). Information Technology’s
forward P/E has dropped from 26.5 at the end of last year to 17.2.
Communication Services’ forward P/E is down from 22.1 to 17.0.

### Page 8

IV. Credit
The 10-year Treasury bond yield jumped to 5.10% after October's CPI and
PPI reports (chart). That’s still below its October peak above 5.30%.
The 10-year yield is hovering near the top end of the 4.00%-5.00% range
we consider normal (chart).

### Page 9

The latest increase in the 10-year nominal yield has been attributable to
the 10-year TIPS yield. The spread between the two, which is a proxy for
10-year expected inflation, remains low at 2.21% (chart). That's surprising
since the recent yield rise followed the hotter-than-expected CPI and PPI
reports.
Credit default swap spreads rose in recent months on concerns about the
debt of CoreWeave and other AI borrowers but have since narrowed
(chart). We don’t see broadening credit stress.

### Page 10

V. Commodities
Commodity prices are signaling less inflationary pressure. The FIBER
Commodities Spot Price Index is looking toppy (chart).
The S&P GSCI Energy Spot Index also seems to be topping out along
with oil prices (chart).

### Page 11

Even diesel prices have come down substantially. The Gulf Coast spot
price fell to $4.11 a gallon from a peak above $5.00 earlier this year
(chart).
Agricultural prices have also eased from their summer highs (chart).

### Page 12

The copper price has been getting hit along with the AI trade. It has fallen
to $6.08 a pound from its recent record high above $6.80 (chart).

## Document 476: インドネシア、ギブラン副大統領の辞任意向表明で政局に新たな波乱 ～プラボウォ・ジョコ両氏の関係変化を背景に、2029年に向けた_d1eb3c06

Source PDF: all report pdf/インドネシア、ギブラン副大統領の辞任意向表明で政局に新たな波乱 ～プラボウォ・ジョコ両氏の関係変化を背景に、2029年に向けた_d1eb3c06.pdf
Report source: DLRI

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年11 月 11 日

1/5 
インドネシア、ギブラン副大統領の辞任意向表明で政局に新たな波乱 
～プラボウォ・ジョコ両氏の関係変化を背景に、2029 年に向けた政治勢力再編が進む可能性～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ インドネシアでは、ギブラン副大統領がプラボウォ大統領に辞任の意向を伝えたことが明ら
かになり、政局に新たな動揺が広がっている。背景には、2024 年の大統領選挙に際して提出
した学歴証明書類の有効性を巡り、司法機関が相次いで同氏に不利な判断を示したことがあ
る。議会でも資格問題を追及する動きが強まるなか、ギブラン氏は法的手段による対応を模
索する一方、問題が政権全体を巻き込む政治的混乱に発展することを回避すべく、辞任の意
向を示したと考えられる。ただし、現時点では正式な辞任には至っていない。 
○ 今回の問題の背景には、プラボウォ大統領とジョコ前大統領の政治的関係の変化が影響して
いる可能性もある。両者は 2024 年の大統領選挙を通じて協力関係を構築したものの、プラボ
ウォ政権の発足後は、閣僚人事や新首都建設を巡る政策面での違いなどから、次第に距離が
生じてきたとされる。さらに、足元ではジョコ氏に近い人物の拘束や、同氏の次男が率いる
政党からの閣僚らの離党が相次ぎ、両者の政治的力学に変化が生じている可能性がある。 
○ ギブラン氏が正式に辞任した場合、後任の副大統領の選定を通じて、プラボウォ政権内部の
勢力関係が変化する可能性がある。幅広い政党による連立体制を背景に、辞任が直ちに政権
の存続を脅かすとは限らず、むしろ後任人事を通じて政権基盤を強化する余地もある。他方、
与党連立内部の利害調整が複雑化する可能性があるほか、ジョコ氏にとっては現政権への重
要な影響力行使の手段を失うことにつながるため、2029 年の次期大統領・議会選挙を見据え
た政治勢力の再編が進むことも考えられる。 
○ 一方、インドネシア金融市場では、中銀の独立性や財政規律への懸念などを背景に、通貨ル
ピアや株式相場への下押し圧力がくすぶっている。こうしたなかで政治的混乱が長期化すれ
ば、政策運営の予見可能性に対する懸念を通じて金融市場の新たな重しとなる可能性がある。
先行きは、ギブラン氏の正式な辞任の有無に加え、プラボウォ政権がジョコ前政権からの政
治的な自立をどこまで進めるのか、そして 2029 年に向けて主要政治勢力の関係がどのよう
に変化するかが重要な焦点となる。

### Page 2

2/5 
【ギブラン副大統領が突然の辞任意向を表明、立候補資格を巡る問題の再燃が背景に】 
インドネシアの政局が新たな動揺に見舞われている。同国政府は 10 日、ギブラン副大統領がプ
ラボウォ大統領と約２時間半にわたって会談し、副大統領職を辞任する意向を伝えたことを明らか
にした。政府側は、プラボウォ氏が辞任の意向を拒否しなかったと説明している。背景には、2024
年の大統領選挙に際して提出した学歴証明書類の有効性を巡り、司法機関が相次いで同氏に不利な
判断を示したことがある。なお、ギブラン氏は辞任の意向を伝える一方、行政裁判所での手続きを
通じて自身の学歴証明書類の有効性を立証する機会を求めているとされる。プラボウォ氏も法的手
段による対応を助言したとされ、現時点では正式な辞任には至っていない。このため、ギブラン氏
は引き続き副大統領として職務を遂行しているものの、今回の動きは、2024 年の大統領選挙を通じ
て形成されたプラボウォ氏とジョコ前大統領の政治的協力関係に変化が生じている可能性を示唆
している。 
今回、ギブラン氏による辞任意向表明に至った背景には、同氏の副大統領への立候補資格を巡る
問題がある。同氏は、2024 年の大統領選挙への出馬に際して、選挙法で定める大統領、副大統領候
補の年齢規定（40 歳以上）を下回る 36 歳であったにもかかわらず、憲法裁判所が地方自治体の首
長経験者について立候補年齢の例外を認めたことで、副大統領候補となることが可能になった。こ
の判断を巡っては、当時の憲法裁判所長官であったアンワル氏がジョコ前大統領の義弟（妹の夫）
に当たるなど利益相反が問題視され、その後、同氏は重大な倫理違反を理由に長官職を解かれた経
緯がある（注１）。しかし、その後も司法判断そのものは無効とはされず、ギブラン氏はプラボウォ
氏とともに大統領選挙に勝利した。このため、同氏の立候補を巡っては、当初から政治的な論争が
続いてきたものの、足元では年齢要件とは別に、学歴要件を巡る問題が浮上している。 
具体的には、10 月６日に憲法裁判所が学歴要件を巡る申し立てを原告適格の問題から不受理と
した一方、ギブラン氏が海外（シンガポールのオーキッド・パーク中等学校、オーストラリアのシ
ドニー工科大学付属インサーチ）で受けた教育について、国内の高校卒業資格に相当することを裏
付ける十分な証拠が確認できないとの見解を示した。さらに、10 月 30 日には最高裁判所が、2024
年の立候補に際して提出された高校卒業資格の同等性を認める証明書を無効とする判断を下した。
こうした司法判断を受けて、議会でもギブラン氏の資格問題を追及する動きが強まっている。国民
代表議会（下院）では、６会派に所属する約 50 人の議員が、弾劾手続きにつながり得る「意見表明
権」の行使を提案したとされる。このため、ギブラン氏としては、法的な争いを継続する姿勢を維
持する一方、自身の進退問題が政権全体を巻き込む政治的混乱に発展することを回避すべく、辞任
の意向を示したと考えられる。

【政局の底流には、プラボウォ大統領とジョコ前大統領の関係悪化懸念が影響している可能性も】 
今回の問題を巡っては、ギブラン氏個人の立候補資格だけでは説明し切れない側面がある。プラ

注１ 2023 年 11 月８日付レポート「インドネシア大統領選､ジョコ氏長男を｢後押し｣した憲法裁所長に辞任命令」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/288141.html）

### Page 3

3/5 
ボウォ氏とジョコ前大統領は、2014 年と 2019 年の大統領選挙では対立候補として激戦を演じた。
しかし、2019 年に発足したジョコ政権２期目では、プラボウォ氏が国防相に登用されたことをきっ
かけに、両者の関係が改善に転じた。さらに、2024 年の大統領選挙では、プラボウォ氏がジョコ氏
の長男であるギブラン氏を副大統領候補に迎え、ジョコ政権の政策を継承する姿勢を示したことで、
ジョコ氏の支持基盤を取り込むことに成功した。このため、プラボウォ政権の発足当初においては、
両者の政治的協力関係が政権基盤の安定化を図る重要な要素となっていた。しかし、政権発足から
時間が経過するにつれ、プラボウォ氏が独自の政策運営を強める一方、内閣改造が実施されるたび
にジョコ前政権の関係者を要職から外す動きがみられた。その結果、両者の関係には次第に距離が
生じてきたとされる。さらに、プラボウォ政権下では、ジョコ前大統領が推進した新首都ヌサンタ
ラの建設予算が抑制されるなど、政策面でも優先順位の違いが表面化している。 
足元では、両者の関係を一段と複雑する動きが顕在化している。国家警察は 11 月初め、ジョコ
氏に近いとされるリスティオ大統領前首席補佐官（前国家警察長官）らを、プラボウォ氏を含む政
府高官などへの違法な通信傍受に関与した疑いで拘束した。この問題を巡っては捜査が続いており、
ジョコ氏などの関与が確認されている訳ではないものの、両者の政治的関係を巡る警戒感を高める
一因となっている。さらに、直後には、ジョコ氏の次男（カエサン氏）が党首を務めるインドネシ
ア連帯党（ＰＳＩ）に所属する現職閣僚や副大臣３名が相次いで離党を表明した。３名は離党の理
由に、プラボウォ政権での職務の専念などを挙げたものの、一連の動きは、政権関係者の間でジョ
コ氏との政治的な距離を見直す動きが生じている可能性を示唆している。その意味では、今回のギ
ブラン氏の辞任意向表明についても、こうした政治的な力学の変化を踏まえて捉える必要がある。

【副大統領辞任なら政権基盤の再編が進む可能性、ジョコ前大統領の動きにも注意】 
今後、ギブラン氏が正式に副大統領職を辞任すれば、プラボウォ氏による政権運営にも一定の変
化が生じると考えられる。現行憲法では、副大統領に欠員が生じた場合、大統領が推薦する２人の
候補者から、国民協議会（国民代表議会と地方代表議会で構成）が 60 日以内に新たな副大統領を
選出すると定められている（第８条第２
項）。このため、ギブラン氏の辞任後は、
後任の選定を巡って与党連立内の調整
が必要となる。プラボウォ政権は幅広い
政党を取り込んだ連立体制を構築して
いるため（図１）、副大統領の辞任が直ち
に政権の存続を脅かすとは限らない。む
しろ、後任人事を通じて大統領の政策運
営に協力的な勢力との結び付きを強め
ることも可能となる。ただし、主要政党
が後任ポストや政策面での影響力拡大
を求めれば、連立内部の利害調整がこれまで以上に複雑化することも予想される。加えて、司法判
図 1 国民議会における各党の議席数

（出所）各種報道より作成, 太字は与党, 斜字は閣外協力

### Page 4

4/5 
断や議会による追及への対応が長期化する場合には、政権による政策遂行に対する政治的な負担が
高まることも考えられる。 
一方で、今回の動きはジョコ前大統領の政治的な立場にも影響を及ぼすと考えられる。同氏は大
統領退任後も高い知名度と一定の支持基盤を維持しており、近年はカエサン氏が率いるＰＳＩを通
じて政治的な活動を活発化させてきた。しかし、ギブラン氏が副大統領を退任すれば、ジョコ氏が
現政権に対して影響力を行使するうえで重要な接点が失われる。他方、ジョコ氏が引き続きＰＳＩ
や自身の支持者組織を通じて政治活動を続ける場合、政権との協力関係を再構築する動きが生じる
可能性があるものの、現政権とは異なる政治的な立場を鮮明にする展開も考えられる。とりわけ、
2029 年に実施予定の次期大統領・議会選挙に向けて、プラボウォ氏を支える既存の与党勢力とジョ
コ氏に近い勢力がどのような関係を築くかは、政治的な争点のひとつになる。ジョコ氏の影響力の
行方を判断するうえでは、ギブラン氏自身の政治活動の継続に加え、ＰＳＩの組織基盤の拡大や既
存政党との協力関係が重要となる。

【政治的混乱が金融市場の新たな重しとなる可能性、2029 年に向けた政局再編にも注目】 
今回の政治的混乱によって、金融市場が大幅な動揺に見舞われるかは不透明である。とはいえ、
足元のインドネシア市場を巡っては、中銀の独立性を巡る懸念などを理由に通貨ルピア安圧力がく
すぶるとともに、指数算出会社のＭＳＣＩが同国を「スタンドアローン市場」に再分類する案につ
いて協議を開始したことなどを受けて
（注２）、その後の主要株価指数（ジャカ
ルタ総合指数）は調整の動きを強めてい
る（図２）。さらに、プラボウォ政権が推
進する無償給食事業などの大幅な財政
出動による悪影響への懸念も、市場の重
しとなっている。こうした状況のなかで
政治的な混乱が長期化すれば、政策運営
の予見可能性や財政規律に対する投資
家の評価を通じて、金融市場に影響が及
ぶ可能性は否定できない。とりわけ、与
党連立内部における対立が予算編成や政権による政策遂行に波及するか否かが重要になる。 
先行きについては、ギブラン氏が司法手続きを通じて自身の立場を維持する場合と、正式な辞任
に踏み切り、新たな副大統領の選出が進む場合の双方を念頭に置く必要がある。前者の場合は、資
格問題を巡る政治的な対立が長期化する可能性があり、後者の場合は、副大統領の後任人事を通じ
て政権内部の勢力関係が変化することが考えられる。ただし、いずれの展開となった場合でも、プ

至るか否かだけでなく、プラボウォ政権がジョコ前政権からの政治的な自立をどこまで進めるのか、
そして 2029 年に向けてインドネシアの主要政治勢力の関係がどのように変化するかが、今後の政
局を見通していくうえで重要な焦点になると考えられる。

## Document 493: タイ・７-９月GDPは外需主導で底堅さをみせるも、先行きは不透明 ～AI・半導体需要が下支えする一方、インフレと異常気象が内需の重しに～

Source PDF: all report pdf/タイ・７-９月GDPは外需主導で底堅さをみせるも、先行きは不透明 ～AI・半導体需要が下支えする一方、インフレと異常気象が内需の重しに～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026年11月 16日

1/4 
タイ・７-９月 GDPは外需主導で底堅さをみせるも、先行きは不透明 
～AI・半導体需要が下支えする一方、インフレと異常気象が内需の重しに～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ タイ経済では、イラン情勢の悪化に伴うエネルギー価格の上昇にバーツ安による輸入物価の
上昇が重なり、インフレ圧力が強まっている。10月のインフレ率は前年同月比＋3.90％と中
銀の目標レンジを上回り、2023年１月以来の高い伸びとなった。バーツ安の背景には、金価
格の調整に加え、原油高による対外収支の悪化や、家計支援策・洪水対策に伴う財政負担の
拡大など、経済のファンダメンタルズの脆弱化に対する懸念がある。 
○ 一方、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の拡大を背景とする財輸出の堅調さや、サービス輸出
の拡大、データセンター関連投資の活発化が景気を下支えしている。７-９月の実質ＧＤＰ成
長率は前年同期比＋2.9％と前期（同＋2.4％）から伸びが加速したものの、前期比では＋0.3％
（年率換算＋1.35％）にとどまり、景気の勢いは力強さを欠いている。個人消費も政府の補
助金政策に支えられるなど、自律的な景気拡大には至っていない。 
○ 政府は2026年の経済成長率を＋2.5％、2027年を＋2.0～3.0％と見込んでいる。先行きもＡ
Ｉ・半導体関連需要が景気を下支えすると期待される一方、インフレの高止まりや政策支援
効果の剥落、データセンター関連投資の鈍化が懸念される。さらに、エルニーニョ現象に伴
う高温・少雨が農業生産や製造業の活動を下押しするとともに、食料品や電力価格の上昇を
通じてインフレ圧力を強める可能性がある。外需の堅調さが景気を支える一方、生活必需品
を中心とするインフレが内需を圧迫し、低成長が長期化するリスクに注意が必要である。

### Page 2

2/4 
【エネルギー価格の上昇にバーツ安が重なり、インフレ圧力が一段と強まる展開】 
アジア新興国は、イラン情勢の悪化に伴う原油高をはじめとするエネルギー価格の上昇が景気の
重しとなることが懸念されている。タイ
では、2025年半ば以降、インフレ率がマ
イナス圏で推移するなど物価下落が続
いてきたものの、2026年４月以降は一転
してプラスに転じている。直近の 10月
のインフレ率は前年同月比＋3.90％と
中銀の目標レンジ（２±１％）を上回り、
2023 年１月以来の高い伸びとなってい
る（図１）。コアインフレ率は前年同月比
＋1.58％と目標レンジ内で推移してい
るものの、2023年４月以来の高い伸びと
なるなど、着実にインフレ圧力が強まる動きがみられる。 
インフレ圧力が広がりをみせている背
景には、金融市場において通貨バーツ相
場が下落基調を強めており、輸入物価の
上昇が幅広い財価格を押し上げているこ
とが影響している。タイでは伝統的に金
の取引が活発であり、バーツ相場は年明
けからしばらくの間、金の国際価格に連
動する形で強含んできた。しかし、足元
では金価格が調整の動きを強めており、
バーツ相場の重しとなっている可能性が
ある。さらに、原油高が輸入額を押し上
げて対外収支を悪化させるとともに、政府による家計支援を目的とする補助金政策が財政負担を膨
らませるなど、経済のファンダメンタルズ（基礎的条件）の脆弱化が警戒されている。そのうえ、
９月中旬以降に豪雨による洪水被害が広がったことを受けて、政府は洪水対策を目的とする財政支
援を拡充しており、公的債務残高は法定上限（ＧＤＰ比 70％）に近付いている（注１）。こうした
事情も、バーツ安の動きが強まる一因になってきたと考えられる。

3/4 
【世界的なAI・半導体関連需要の拡大やサービス輸出の堅調さが景気を下支え】 
一方、世界的にはＡＩ（人工知能）・半導体関連需要の旺盛な動きが続いている。関連産業が集積
する韓国や台湾のほかタイを含むＡＳＥＡＮ（東南アジア諸国連合）諸国では、半導体など電子部
品関連の輸出が押し上げられている。さらに、データセンター関連投資も拡大しており、景気を下
支えする。その結果、イラン情勢の悪化
に伴う悪影響が、世界的なＡＩ・半導体
関連需要の旺盛さによる好影響によって
一定程度相殺されている。このように、
足元のタイ経済にはマイナス、プラス双
方の材料が混在するなか、７-９月の実質
ＧＤＰ成長率は前年同期比＋2.9％と前
期（同＋2.4％）から加速、持ち直しが確
認された。ただし、前期比の成長率は＋
0.3％（年率換算＋1.35％）と前期（同＋
0.07％）から伸びが加速しているものの、
依然として力強さを欠く推移が続いている（図３）。 
イラン情勢の悪化に伴うインフレの加速を受けて、家計の実質購買力には下押し圧力がかかって
いるものの、政府による家計支援を目的とする補助金政策が個人消費を下支えしている。また、世
界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の旺盛さが
半導体を中心とする財輸出を押し上げ
ている。一方、外国人観光客数が低迷す
るなかでもサービス輸出は拡大が続い
ており、外需が景気をけん引している。
加えて、活発なデータセンター関連投資
が固定資本投資を押し上げている。個人
消費や設備投資が底堅く推移する一方、
輸入は力強さを欠いており、純輸出の成
長率寄与度を押し上げる要因となった。
また、前期は在庫投資の成長率寄与度が
大幅なプラスとなり、成長率が示す以上に景気の実態は弱い内容となったものの、当期は一転して
大幅なマイナスと試算されるなど、在庫調整が進んでいる様子がうかがえる（図４）。 
分野ごとの生産動向を巡っては、異常気象の頻発などを理由に農林漁業の生産が下振れしたほか、
鉱業部門の生産も弱含んだ。一方、半導体関連を中心とする輸出の堅調さを反映して製造業の生産
が押し上げられたほか、政策支援による個人消費の下支えを受けてサービス業の生産も拡大してい
る。ただし、サービス業のうち不動産関連や教育関連などの活動は低迷しており、政策支援の有無
によって内需の動向に明暗が分かれるなど、自律的な景気拡大にはほど遠い状況にあると考えられ
図 3 実質 GDP(季節調整値)と成長率(前年比)の推移

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告

りません。
